Abstract: Foundation models in general promise to accelerate scientific computation by learning reusable representations across problem instances, yet constrained scientific systems, where predictions must satisfy physical laws and safety limits, pose unique challenges that stress conventional training paradigms. We derive design principles for constrained scientific foundation models through systematic investigation of AC optimal power flow (ACOPF), a representative optimization problem in power grid operations where power balance equations and operational constraints are non-negotiable. Through controlled experiments spanning architectures, training objectives, and system diversity, we extract three empirically grounded principles governing scientific foundation model design. These principles characterize three design trade-offs: learning physics-invariant representations while respecting system-specific constraints, optimizing accuracy while ensuring constraint satisfaction, and ensuring reliability in high-impact operating regimes. We present the LUMINA framework, including data processing and training pipelines to support reproducible research on physics-informed, feasibility-aware foundation models across scientific applications.

</details>


### [119] [Activation Outliers in Transformer Quantization: Reproduction, Statistical Analysis, and Deployment Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04308)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BERT-base量化后精度大幅下降35.33%，主要原因是深层激活的严重重尾分布和通道级异常值，混合精度量化能恢复接近FP32精度，而百分比校准无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Transformer模型后训练量化中严重的精度下降问题，特别是由结构化激活异常值引起的量化失效现象，旨在通过实证分析理解其根本原因并评估不同缓解策略的有效性。

Method: 对BERT-base模型在QNLI任务上进行全局W8A8量化，分析FP32激活的统计特性（峰度、能量分布），评估混合精度量化、按嵌入组量化和百分比校准三种缓解策略，并在RTX 3050 GPU上进行部署性能分析。

Result: 量化后精度从89.66%降至54.33%（下降35.33%）。深层激活峰度达271，55%能量集中在1%通道。混合精度量化恢复至89.42%，按嵌入组量化从66.12%（3组）提升至86.18%（4组），百分比校准无效（约50.54%）。硬件性能差异微小（延迟58-59ms，VRAM 484-486MB）。

Conclusion: Transformer量化失效主要由残差连接放大的结构化通道主导引起，有效缓解需要通道感知的精度分配而非简单的标量截断，混合精度量化是最有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of transformers is known to suffer from severe accuracy degradation due to structured activation outliers, as originally analyzed by Bondarenko et al. (EMNLP 2021) in work associated with Qualcomm AI Research. This paper provides a reproducible empirical reproduction and systems-level extension of that phenomenon in BERT-base fine-tuned on QNLI. When global W8A8 quantization is applied, validation accuracy drops sharply from 89.66% (FP32) to 54.33%, a decrease of 35.33 points. Statistical analysis of FP32 activations shows strongly heavy-tailed behavior that intensifies with model depth: kurtosis reaches 271 in the final layers and approximately 55% of activation energy is concentrated in the top 1% of channels. We evaluate several mitigation strategies. Mixed precision PTQ restores accuracy close to the FP32 baseline (89.42%). Per-embedding-group (PEG) quantization shows strong sensitivity to grouping structure, improving accuracy from 66.12% with three groups to 86.18% with four groups. In contrast, percentile-based calibration, even at thresholds between 99.0 and 99.99, fails to recover accuracy (about 50.54%), indicating that large activation channels encode structured signal rather than rare noise. Deployment profiling on an RTX 3050 GPU shows minimal differences in latency and memory usage across methods (median latency about 58-59 ms; VRAM usage about 484-486 MB), highlighting the importance of hardware-aware evaluation. Overall, the results show that PTQ failure in transformers is primarily driven by structured channel dominance amplified through residual connections. Effective mitigation therefore requires channel-aware precision allocation rather than scalar clipping alone.

</details>


### [120] [CRESTomics: Analyzing Carotid Plaques in the CREST-2 Trial with a New Additive Classification Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04309)
*Pranav Kulkarni,Brajesh K. Lal,Georges Jreij,Sai Vallamchetla,Langford Green,Jenifer Voeks,John Huston,Lloyd Edwards,George Howard,Bradley A. Maron,Thomas G. Brott,James F. Meschia,Florence X. Doo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于核的加性模型，结合相干损失和组稀疏正则化，从B超图像中识别与高风险相关的放射组学标志物，用于颈动脉斑块评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈动脉斑块的准确表征对卒中预防至关重要，需要从B超图像中识别与高风险相关的放射组学标志物，但现有方法在准确性和可解释性方面存在局限

Method: 提出新的基于核的加性模型，结合相干损失与组稀疏正则化进行非线性分类，使用偏依赖图可视化各特征组的加性效应

Result: 方法能够准确且可解释地评估斑块，揭示了斑块纹理特征与临床风险之间的强关联

Conclusion: 提出的核加性模型为颈动脉斑块风险评估提供了一种准确且可解释的放射组学分析方法，有助于卒中预防

Abstract: Accurate characterization of carotid plaques is critical for stroke prevention in patients with carotid stenosis. We analyze 500 plaques from CREST-2, a multi-center clinical trial, to identify radiomics-based markers from B-mode ultrasound images linked with high-risk. We propose a new kernel-based additive model, combining coherence loss with group-sparse regularization for nonlinear classification. Group-wise additive effects of each feature group are visualized using partial dependence plots. Results indicate our method accurately and interpretably assesses plaques, revealing a strong association between plaque texture and clinical risk.

</details>


### [121] [PTOPOFL: Privacy-Preserving Personalised Federated Learning via Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04323)
*Kelly L Vomo-Donfack,Adryel Hoszu,Grégory Ginot,Ian Morilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PTOPOFL：一种联邦学习框架，用持久同调拓扑描述符替代梯度共享，同时解决隐私攻击和非IID数据分布问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临两个结构性矛盾：梯度共享导致数据重构攻击风险，而非IID客户端分布降低聚合质量。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出PTOPOFL框架，用持久同调(PH)拓扑描述符替代梯度传输。客户端仅传输48维PH特征向量（紧凑的形状摘要），服务器基于Wasserstein距离进行拓扑引导的个性化聚合：客户端按PH图相似性聚类，簇内模型进行拓扑加权，簇间与全局共识融合。

Result: 在非IID医疗场景（8家医院，2个对抗性）和病理基准测试（10个客户端）中，PTOPOFL分别达到AUC 0.841和0.910，均优于FedAvg、FedProx、SCAFFOLD和pFedMe。重构风险比梯度共享降低4.5倍。

Conclusion: PTOPOFL通过拓扑描述符同时解决了联邦学习的隐私和聚合质量问题，在保持高性能的同时显著降低隐私风险，为联邦学习提供了新的隐私保护聚合范式。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) faces two structural tensions: gradient sharing enables data-reconstruction attacks, while non-IID client distributions degrade aggregation quality. We introduce PTOPOFL, a framework that addresses both challenges simultaneously by replacing gradient communication with topological descriptors derived from persistent homology (PH). Clients transmit only 48-dimensional PH feature vectors-compact shape summaries whose many-to-one structure makes inversion provably ill-posed-rather than model gradients. The server performs topology-guided personalised aggregation: clients are clustered by Wasserstein similarity between their PH diagrams, intra-cluster models are topology-weighted,and clusters are blended with a global consensus. We prove an information-contraction theorem showing that PH descriptors leak strictly less mutual information per sample than gradients under strongly convex loss functions, and we establish linear convergence of the Wasserstein-weighted aggregation scheme with an error floor strictly smaller than FedAvg. Evaluated against FedAvg, FedProx, SCAFFOLD, and pFedMe on a non-IID healthcare scenario (8 hospitals, 2 adversarial) and a pathological benchmark (10 clients), PTOPOFL achieves AUC 0.841 and 0.910 respectively-the highest in both settings-while reducing reconstruction risk by a factor of 4.5 relative to gradient sharing. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MorillaLab/TopoFederatedL and data at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18827595.

</details>


### [122] [Algorithmic Compliance and Regulatory Loss in Digital Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04328)
*Khem Raj Bhatt,Krishna Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 机器学习加密货币反洗钱系统在静态评估中表现良好，但在实际部署中因时间非平稳性导致监管效果被高估，产生持续超额损失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机器学习在加密货币反洗钱执法系统中的实际部署性能，揭示静态分类指标如何高估真实监管效果，强调在动态数字资产市场中固定执法策略的脆弱性

Method: 使用比特币交易数据进行前瞻性和滚动评估，分析时间非平稳性对成本敏感执法阈值的影响，比较动态最优基准与实际部署性能

Result: 强静态分类指标显著高估实际监管效果；时间非平稳性导致执法阈值不稳定，产生持续超额监管损失；核心失败源于决策规则校准错误而非预测准确性下降

Conclusion: 固定反洗钱执法策略在演化的数字资产市场中具有脆弱性，需要基于损失的评估框架进行监管监督，强调动态调整决策规则的重要性

Abstract: We study the deployment performance of machine learning based enforcement systems used in cryptocurrency anti money laundering (AML). Using forward looking and rolling evaluations on Bitcoin transaction data, we show that strong static classification metrics substantially overstate real world regulatory effectiveness. Temporal nonstationarity induces pronounced instability in cost sensitive enforcement thresholds, generating large and persistent excess regulatory losses relative to dynamically optimal benchmarks. The core failure arises from miscalibration of decision rules rather than from declining predictive accuracy per se. These findings underscore the fragility of fixed AML enforcement policies in evolving digital asset markets and motivate loss-based evaluation frameworks for regulatory oversight.

</details>


### [123] [Out-of-distribution transfer of PDE foundation models to material dynamics under extreme loading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04354)
*Mahindra Rautela,Alexander Most,Siddharth Mansingh,Aleksandra Pachalieva,Bradley Love,Daniel O Malley,Alexander Scheinker,Kyle Hickmann,Diane Oyen,Nathan Debardeleben,Earl Lawrence,Ayan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估PDE基础模型在极端载荷材料动力学中的外推能力，重点关注冲击驱动多材料界面动力学和动态断裂两种不连续主导的物理场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数PDE基础模型在流体中心基准上进行预训练和微调，其在极端载荷材料动力学（涉及冲击波、界面演化和断裂等高度非光滑场）中的实用性尚不明确

Method: 将下游任务制定为终端状态预测，即学习从第一个快照直接预测最终状态的长时程映射。使用统一的训练和评估协议，评估POSEIDON和MORPH两个开源预训练PDE基础模型，比较从预训练权重微调与从头训练在不同训练集规模下的表现

Result: 通过系统实验量化了在分布偏移下的样本效率，揭示了预训练模型在极端材料动力学场景中的外推能力

Conclusion: 该研究为评估PDE基础模型在不连续主导的极端物理场景中的泛化能力提供了基准，有助于理解预训练策略在材料动力学中的有效性

Abstract: Most PDE foundation models are pretrained and fine-tuned on fluid-centric benchmarks. Their utility under extreme-loading material dynamics remains unclear. We benchmark out-of-distribution transfer on two discontinuity-dominated regimes in which shocks, evolving interfaces, and fracture produce highly non-smooth fields: shock-driven multi-material interface dynamics (perturbed layered interface or PLI) and dynamic fracture/failure evolution (FRAC). We formulate the downstream task as terminal-state prediction, i.e., learning a long-horizon map that predicts the final state directly from the first snapshot without intermediate supervision. Using a unified training and evaluation protocol, we evaluate two open-source pretrained PDE foundation models, POSEIDON and MORPH, and compare fine-tuning from pretrained weights against training from scratch across training-set sizes to quantify sample efficiency under distribution shift.

</details>


### [124] [Dissecting Quantization Error: A Concentration-Alignment Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04359)
*Marco Federici,Boris van Breugel,Paul Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出块级集中对齐变换(CAT)，通过联合优化权重和激活的集中度与对齐度来最大化信噪比，从而降低4位量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数保持变换（如旋转、哈达玛变换）主要关注降低权重和激活的集中度（减少离群值），但缺乏理论解释。作者发现信噪比(SQNR)不仅取决于集中度，还取决于权重和激活主导变化方向的对齐度，这为减少量化误差提供了新的优化维度。

Method: 通过信噪比(SQNR)分析线性层量化，发现SQNR可分解为：(1)权重和激活的集中度（捕获分布范围和离群值），(2)它们主导变化方向的对齐度。基于此提出块级集中对齐变换(CAT)，利用小型校准集的协方差估计来联合优化集中度和对齐度，近似最大化SQNR。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，CAT在4位精度下持续匹配或优于先前的基于变换的量化方法，验证了理论框架的洞察。

Conclusion: 量化误差不仅受权重和激活集中度影响，还受其对齐度影响。CAT通过联合优化这两个因素有效降低量化误差，为后训练量化提供了新的理论指导和实用方法。

Abstract: Quantization can drastically increase the efficiency of large language and vision models, but typically incurs an accuracy drop. Recently, function-preserving transforms (e.g. rotations, Hadamard transform, channel-wise scaling) have been successfully applied to reduce post-training quantization error, yet a principled explanation remains elusive. We analyze linear-layer quantization via the signal-to-quantization-noise ratio (SQNR), showing that for uniform integer quantization at a fixed bit width, SQNR decomposes into (i) the concentration of weights and activations (capturing spread and outliers), and (ii) the alignment of their dominant variation directions. This reveals an actionable insight: beyond concentration - the focus of most prior transforms (e.g. rotations or Hadamard) - improving alignment between weight and activation can further reduce quantization error. Motivated by this, we introduce block Concentration-Alignment Transforms (CAT), a lightweight linear transformation that uses a covariance estimate from a small calibration set to jointly improve concentration and alignment, approximately maximizing SQNR. Experiments across several LLMs show that CAT consistently matches or outperforms prior transform-based quantization methods at 4-bit precision, confirming the insights gained in our framework.

</details>


### [125] [Robustness of Agentic AI Systems via Adversarially-Aligned Jacobian Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04378)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AAJR是一种对抗对齐的雅可比正则化方法，用于稳定多智能体LLM的极小极大训练，通过仅沿对抗上升方向控制敏感性来减少全局约束的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型向自主多智能体生态系统过渡，鲁棒的极小极大训练变得至关重要。然而，当高度非线性策略在内部最大化中引发极端局部曲率时，这种训练容易变得不稳定。标准的全局雅可比边界方法过于保守，抑制了所有方向的敏感性，导致较大的鲁棒性代价。

Method: 提出了对抗对齐的雅可比正则化（AAJR），这是一种轨迹对齐方法，严格沿对抗上升方向控制敏感性。该方法在温和条件下证明能产生比全局约束更大的可容许策略类别，意味着更小的近似差距和减少的名义性能退化。此外，推导了步长条件，确保AAJR沿优化轨迹控制有效平滑度并保证内环稳定性。

Result: AAJR提供了一种结构理论，将极小极大稳定性与全局表达能力限制解耦。该方法在温和条件下产生比全局约束更大的可容许策略类别，意味着更小的近似差距和减少的名义性能退化。推导的步长条件确保沿优化轨迹的有效平滑度控制和内环稳定性。

Conclusion: AAJR为智能体鲁棒性提供了一种结构理论，通过轨迹对齐方法控制对抗方向的敏感性，避免了全局约束的过度保守性，实现了极小极大稳定性与全局表达能力限制的解耦，从而在多智能体LLM训练中实现更优的鲁棒性-性能权衡。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition into autonomous multi-agent ecosystems, robust minimax training becomes essential yet remains prone to instability when highly non-linear policies induce extreme local curvature in the inner maximization. Standard remedies that enforce global Jacobian bounds are overly conservative, suppressing sensitivity in all directions and inducing a large Price of Robustness. We introduce Adversarially-Aligned Jacobian Regularization (AAJR), a trajectory-aligned approach that controls sensitivity strictly along adversarial ascent directions. We prove that AAJR yields a strictly larger admissible policy class than global constraints under mild conditions, implying a weakly smaller approximation gap and reduced nominal performance degradation. Furthermore, we derive step-size conditions under which AAJR controls effective smoothness along optimization trajectories and ensures inner-loop stability. These results provide a structural theory for agentic robustness that decouples minimax stability from global expressivity restrictions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Distributed Parallel Structure-Aware Presolving for Arrowhead Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03498)
*Nils-Christian Kempke,Stephen J Maher,Daniel Rehfeldt,Ambros Gleixner,Thorsten Koch,Svenja Uslu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出针对箭形线性规划的结构感知并行预求解框架，集成于并行内点法求解器PIPS-IPM++中，在HPC环境下实现高效分布式预求解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模线性规划问题常包含冗余和数值病态，现有预求解技术多为串行或结构无关，在并行求解流程中耗时严重，需要专门针对箭形结构的高性能并行预求解方法

Method: 基于PIPS-IPM++的分布式内存存储，设计结构感知的并行预求解框架，保持箭形结构的同时实现跨计算节点的高度并行分布式预求解，最小化通信开销

Result: 在单机上比PaPILO快18倍，比Gurobi预求解快6倍（移位几何平均运行时间），问题缩减程度与PaPILO相当；在分布式环境中比Gurobi预求解快13倍

Conclusion: 提出的结构感知并行预求解框架在箭形线性规划上表现出卓越的扩展性和效率，显著优于现有最先进的预求解实现，适用于HPC环境

Abstract: We present a structure-aware parallel presolve framework specialized to arrowhead linear programs (AHLPs) and designed for high-performance computing (HPC) environments, integrated into the parallel interior point solver PIPS-IPM++. Large-scale LPs arising from automated model generation frequently contain redundancies and numerical pathologies that necessitate effective presolve, yet existing presolve techniques are primarily serial or structure-agnostic and can become time-consuming in parallel solution workflows.
  Within PIPS-IPM++, AHLPs are stored in distributed memory, and our presolve builds on this to apply a highly parallel, distributed presolve across compute nodes while keeping communication overhead low and preserving the underlying arrowhead structure. We demonstrate the scalability and effectiveness of our approach on a diverse set of AHLPs and compare it against state-of-the-art presolve implementations, including PaPILO and the presolve implemented within Gurobi. Even on a single machine, our presolve significantly outperforms PaPILO by a factor of 18 and Gurobi's presolve by a factor of 6 in terms of shifted geometric mean runtime, while reducing the problems by a similar amount to PaPILO. Using a distributed compute environment, we outperform Gurobi's presolve by a factor of 13.

</details>


### [127] [Negative Curvature Methods with High-Probability Complexity Guarantees for Stochastic Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03625)
*Albert S. Berahas,Raghu Bollapragada,Wanping Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于随机环境下连续非线性无约束优化的负曲率方法框架，通过结合梯度步和负曲率步，在概率Oracle提供近似函数、梯度和Hessian信息的情况下，获得二阶驻点的高概率迭代复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机优化中仅能通过概率Oracle获得近似函数、梯度和Hessian信息的问题，特别是在存在噪声的情况下如何有效利用负曲率方向加速收敛到二阶驻点。

Method: 设计了一个两步框架，系统结合梯度步和负曲率步。采用提前停止机制保证足够进展，使用基于Armijo型准则的自适应机制选择两种步的步长。定义了概率Oracle的条件，并建立了高概率迭代复杂度保证。

Result: 建立了达到二阶驻点的高概率迭代复杂度保证，推导了显式尾界量化收敛邻域及其对Oracle噪声的依赖关系。这些界与确定性速率匹配（仅差噪声相关项），且框架将确定性结果作为特例恢复。数值实验证明了即使在噪声存在下利用负曲率方向的实际优势。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将负曲率方法扩展到随机优化设置，在概率Oracle下保持了确定性方法的收敛性质，为随机非凸优化提供了有效的二阶方法，即使在噪声环境下也能从负曲率信息中获益。

Abstract: This paper develops negative curvature methods for continuous nonlinear unconstrained optimization in stochastic settings, in which function, gradient, and Hessian information is available only through probabilistic oracles, i.e., oracles that return approximations of a certain accuracy and reliability. We introduce conditions on these oracles and design a two-step framework that systematically combines gradient and negative curvature steps. The framework employs an early-stopping mechanism to guarantee sufficient progress and uses an adaptive mechanism based on an Armijo-type criterion to select the step sizes for both steps. We establish high-probability iteration-complexity guarantees for attaining second-order stationary points, deriving explicit tail bounds that quantify the convergence neighborhood and its dependence on oracle noise. Importantly, these bounds match deterministic rates up to noise-dependent terms, and the framework recovers the deterministic results as a special case. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the practical benefits of exploiting negative curvature directions even in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [128] [Frequency Security-Aware Production Scheduling of Utility-Scale Off-Grid Renewable P2H Systems Coordinating Heterogeneous Electrolyzers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03685)
*Jie Zhu,Yiwei Qiu,Yangjun Zeng,Shahab Dehghan,Sheng Wang,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种频率安全感知的离网可再生电制氢系统生产调度统一协同优化框架，协调异构电解槽在满足频率安全约束下最大化氢产量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离网可再生电制氢系统因变流器主导特性而呈现低惯性，频率安全成为关键问题。电解槽虽能参与频率调节，但其支持能力受运行状态和负载水平影响，导致氢产量与频率安全之间存在权衡矛盾。

Method: 建立系统级频率响应模型，捕捉碱性水电解槽、质子交换膜电解槽及其他资源的多阶段频率调节能力；推导分阶段暂态频率安全约束，将其转化为可处理形式并嵌入生产调度；协调电解槽的启停切换和负荷分配，在不确定可再生能源输入下最大化氢产量同时满足频率安全约束。

Result: 案例研究表明，所提方法可使氢厂分别替代55.52%和96.85%来自风力发电机和氨燃料发电机的频率调节储备，同时保持相当的氢产量；全年仿真显示，由于减少对传统储备的依赖，年均净利润增加28.96%。

Conclusion: 该统一协同优化框架有效解决了离网可再生电制氢系统中氢产量与频率安全之间的权衡问题，通过协调异构电解槽的多阶段频率调节能力，在确保系统频率安全的同时最大化氢产量，显著提升系统经济性。

Abstract: Renewable power-to-hydrogen (ReP2H) enables large-scale renewable energy utilization and supports the decarbonization of hard-to-abate sectors, such as chemicals and maritime transport, via hydrogen-based renewable ammonia and methanol fuels. As a result, utility-scale ReP2H projects are expanding worldwide. However, off-grid ReP2H systems exhibit low inertia due to their converter-dominated nature, making frequency security a critical concern. Although recent studies show that electrolyzers can contribute to frequency regulation (FR), their support capability depends on operating states and loading levels, creating a trade-off between hydrogen output and frequency security. To address this challenge, this work develops a unified co-optimization framework for frequency security-aware production scheduling of utility-scale off-grid ReP2H systems coordinating heterogeneous electrolyzers. A system-level frequency response model is established to capture multi-stage FR from alkaline water electrolyzers (AWEs), proton exchange membrane electrolyzers (PEMELs), and other resources, including ammonia-fueled generators retrofitted in co-located chemical plants, battery energy storage, and wind turbines (WTs). Stage-wise transient frequency security constraints are derived, reformulated into tractable forms, and embedded into production scheduling, enabling coordinated on/off switching and load allocation across electrolyzers to maximize hydrogen output under uncertain renewable power input while enforcing frequency security constraints. Case studies based on real-world systems demonstrate that the proposed approach allows HPs to replace 55.52% and 96.85% of FR reserves from WTs and AFGs, respectively, while maintaining comparable hydrogen output. Year-long simulations show an average 28.96% increase in annual net profit resulting from reduced reliance on conventional reserves.

</details>


### [129] [Tractable infinite-dimensional model for long-term environmental impact assessment of long-memory processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03713)
*Hidekazu Yoshioka,Kunihiko Hamagami*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个可解析求解且不发散的环境指数，用于评估具有长记忆特性的底栖藻类暴发过程，通过无限维扩展HJB系统求解，并应用于河流环境中的藻类种群动态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境评估方法在处理具有长记忆特性的过程（如底栖藻类暴发）时面临挑战，特别是在模型不确定性和非指数衰减条件下，需要开发一个既可解析求解又不发散的环境影响评估框架。

Method: 提出基于无限叠加多尺度过程生成长记忆的环境指数框架，通过相对熵和非指数折扣控制敏感性，将环境指数评估转化为求解无限维扩展Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman系统，使用量化技术进行数值处理。

Result: 建立了可解析求解的环境指数框架，能够在充分条件下保证解的唯一存在性和正则性，成功应用于基于实验室实验的河流环境中底栖藻类种群动态评估。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个可处理的框架，用于评估持久性环境现象，特别是在模型不确定性和长记忆过程条件下，为环境风险评估提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: Focusing on the assessment of benthic algae blooms that decay subexponentially, we propose a tractable (solvable in a closed form) and well-defined (that does not diverge) environmental index for the impact assessment of long-memory processes under model uncertainties. Our target system generates long memory through an infinite superposition of multiscale processes. The sensitivity of the environmental index can be controlled by the degree of model uncertainty in terms of the relative entropy and nonexponential discount; hence, we apply a long-memory discount to evaluate long-memory processes. In our framework, the evaluation of the environmental index is reduced to finding a proper solution to an infinite-dimensional extended Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman system. We can solve this system under sufficient conditions for the unique existence of sufficiently regular solutions, and numerically handle them by using a quantization technique. Finally, we present a demonstrative application of the proposed framework to benthic algae population dynamics in river environments based on a laboratorial experiment. This paper offers a tractable framework towards the assessment of persistent environmental phenomena.

</details>


### [130] [A successive difference-of-convex method for a class of two-stage nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic conic program via SVI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03787)
*Chao Zhang,Di Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种求解两阶段非凸非光滑随机锥规划问题的SDC方法，通过KKT条件转化为两阶段随机变分不等式，利用Moreau包络和渐进对冲算法求解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段非凸非光滑随机锥规划问题在应用中广泛存在，但求解面临多重挑战：两阶段结构、场景数量大、非凸非光滑甚至非Lipschitz不连续项、锥约束等，需要开发有效的求解方法

Method: 1) 定义问题的KKT点并证明其为温和条件下的必要最优性条件；2) 将KKT条件转化为等价的非单调非光滑两阶段随机变分不等式；3) 提出SDC方法，利用Moreau包络求解；4) 子问题采用渐进对冲方法求解极大单调两阶段SVI

Result: 在适当假设下证明了SDC方法的严格收敛性，并通过扩展的Markowitz均值-方差模型作为应用实例，数值结果验证了模型和SDC方法的有效性

Conclusion: 成功开发了求解两阶段非凸非光滑随机锥规划问题的SDC方法，将KKT条件转化为SVI并利用Moreau包络和渐进对冲算法求解，理论证明收敛性并通过数值实验验证有效性

Abstract: We consider a class of two-stage nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic conic program, where the objective functions in both stages can contain nonsmooth terms that are functions with easily computed proximal mappings, further composed with affine mappings. This kind of problem is capable of modeling various applications. Solving these problems, however, can be challenging due to the two-stage structure with possibly large number of scenarios, the nonconvex, nonsmooth and even non-Lipschitz discontinuous terms, as well as the conic constraints. In this paper, we define a KKT point of the problem, show that it is a necessary optimality condition under mild conditions, and transform it to an equivalent nonmonotone nonsmooth two-stage stochastic variational inequality (SVI). We then propose a successive difference-of-convex (SDC) method by making use of Moreau envelope to solve it, the subproblems of which are approximately solved by the progressive hedging method for solving maximal monotone two-stage SVI. We show the rigorous convergence of our method under suitable assumptions. An extension of Markowitz's mean-variance model is provided as an application and numerical results on it demonstrate the effectiveness of the model and the SDC method.

</details>


### [131] [Plug-and-Play blind super-resolution of real MRI images for improved multiple sclerosis diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03876)
*Matteo Cannas,Alice Mariottini,Luca Massacesi,Federica Porta,Simone Rebegoldi,Andrea Sebastiani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出盲超分辨率框架增强临床1.5T MRI图像，通过联合估计高分辨率图像和模糊核，使用即插即用去噪器进行图像正则化，实验显示能改善结构定义和临床特征可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床MRI诊断多发性硬化等疾病需要高分辨率图像识别中央静脉征等生物标志物，但大多数临床脑MRI使用1.5T扫描仪，相比高场系统灵敏度较低，且临床环境中只有后处理数据可用，退化模型不完全已知。

Method: 提出盲超分辨率框架，将问题表述为非凸盲逆问题，联合估计高分辨率图像和模糊核。采用即插即用策略，基于预训练去噪器进行图像正则化，对模糊核施加适当约束。设计异构交替块坐标方法，使用不同类型算法更新两个变量，并严格建立收敛性。

Result: 在1.5T采集的FLAIR和SWI序列上实验，显示改善的结构定义和增强的临床相关特征可见性，与3T图像进行视觉比较验证效果。

Conclusion: 提出的盲超分辨率框架能有效增强临床1.5T MRI图像质量，改善临床特征可见性，为临床诊断提供更好的图像支持。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to the diagnosis of multiple sclerosis, where the identification of biomarkers such as the central vein sign benefits from high-resolution images. However, most clinical brain MRI scans are performed using 1.5 T scanners, which provide lower sensitivity compared to higher-field systems. We propose a blind super-resolution framework to enhance real 1.5 T MRI images acquired in clinical settings, where only post-processed data are available and the degradation model is not fully known. The problem is formulated as a non-convex blind inverse problem involving the joint estimation of the high-resolution image and the blur kernel. Image regularization is handled through a Plug-and-Play strategy based on a pretrained denoiser, while suitable constraints are imposed on the blur kernel. To solve the resulting model, we design a heterogeneous alternating block-coordinate method in which the two variables are updated using different types of algorithms. Convergence properties are rigorously established. Experiments on FLAIR and SWI sequences acquired at 1.5 T show improved structural definition and enhanced visibility of clinically relevant features, with visual comparison against 3 T images.

</details>


### [132] [An Adaptive KKT-Based Indicator for Convergence Assessment in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04053)
*Thiago Santos,Sebastiao Xavier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分位数归一化的鲁棒自适应KKT收敛指标，用于多目标优化算法评估，解决传统参考指标在超多目标场景下的可扩展性和参数敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于参考集的指标（如超体积和反世代距离）在超多目标优化中存在可扩展性限制和参数敏感性，而基于KKT最优性条件的指标提供了不依赖外部参考集的内在替代方案，但原始KKT指标对平稳性残差的异质分布敏感。

Method: 重新审视基于熵的KKT收敛指标，提出基于分位数归一化的鲁棒自适应重构。该方法保持原始公式的平稳性解释，同时通过分位数归一化技术提高对平稳性残差异质分布的鲁棒性。

Result: 提出的自适应指标在保持KKT平稳性解释的同时，显著提高了对超多目标优化中常见的平稳性残差异质分布的鲁棒性，解决了原始指标在此类场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于分位数归一化的自适应KKT收敛指标为多目标优化算法评估提供了更鲁棒的内在收敛度量，特别适用于超多目标场景，克服了传统参考指标和原始KKT指标的局限性。

Abstract: Performance indicators are essential tools for assessing the convergence behavior of multi-objective optimization algorithms, particularly when the true Pareto front is
  unknown or difficult to approximate. Classical reference-based metrics such as
  hypervolume and inverted generational distance are widely used, but may suffer from
  scalability limitations and sensitivity to parameter choices in many-objective scenarios.
  Indicators derived from Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions provide an
  intrinsic alternative by quantifying stationarity without relying on external reference
  sets. This paper revisits an entropy-inspired KKT-based convergence indicator and proposes a
  robust adaptive reformulation based on quantile normalization. The proposed indicator
  preserves the stationarity-based interpretation of the original formulation while
  improving robustness to heterogeneous distributions of stationarity residuals, a
  recurring issue in many-objective optimization.

</details>


### [133] [Online Order Fulfillment with Replenishment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04065)
*Zi Ling,Jiashuo Jiang,Linwei Xin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究电商运营中库存补货与实时订单履行的交互作用，通过理论分析和算法设计比较两者对系统性能的相对重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电商和服务运营中，企业需要在需求不确定下联合管理库存补货和实时订单履行以最大化利润。虽然这两个组件各自已被广泛研究，但它们的交互作用仍未被充分探索。本文研究一个基本运营问题：在整体系统性能中，哪个杠杆起更决定性作用——补货还是履行？

Method: 将系统建模为具有销售损失和随机客户到达的单地点在线订单履行问题，每个客户提供异质回报。补货采用基库存或恒定订单策略，实时履行决策使用在线算法。核心性能指标是每个补货周期的预期平均利润。开发了基于遗憾的框架来定量比较改进补货与改进履行的价值，并引入了考虑未来补货和需求的前瞻性在线算法。

Result: 主要理论结果表明，当补货周期较长时，在线履行的累积遗憾与相应单周期问题保持相同数量级，即使在重复补货下也显示出遗憾稳定性。该现象也扩展到多地点设置。研究还刻画了优化补货比改进在线履行算法产生更大收入影响的制度（反之亦然）。数值实验验证了前瞻性算法优于短视基准算法。

Conclusion: 研究结果为库存补货政策何时更具影响力提供了理论和管理的洞察，帮助企业在补货和履行优化之间做出明智的资源分配决策。

Abstract: In modern e-commerce and service operations, firms must jointly manage inventory replenishment and real-time order fulfillment to maximize profit under demand uncertainty. While each component has been studied extensively in isolation, their interaction remains underexplored. This paper investigates a fundamental operational question: which lever plays a more decisive role in overall system performance, replenishment or fulfillment?
  We model the system as a one-location online order fulfillment problem with lost sales and stochastic customer arrivals, each offering heterogeneous rewards. Replenishment follows either a base-stock or constant-order policy, while real-time fulfillment decisions are made using online algorithms. Our core performance metric is the expected average profit per replenishment cycle, evaluated across all combinations of these policies and algorithms. Our main theoretical result shows that when the replenishment cycle is long, the cumulative regret of online fulfillment remains of the same order as in a corresponding single-cycle problem, even under repeated replenishment, revealing a form of regret stability. This phenomenon also extends to a multi-location setting. We further develop a regret-based framework that quantitatively compares the value of improving replenishment versus improving fulfillment, and we characterize regimes in which optimizing replenishment yields a larger revenue impact than refining the online fulfillment algorithm (and vice versa). Motivated by examples where myopic algorithms underperform, we introduce a novel look-ahead online algorithm that anticipates future replenishment and demand. Numerical experiments verify that this algorithm outperforms myopic baselines. Overall, our results provide both theoretical and managerial insights into situations where inventory replenishment policies are more influential and vice versa.

</details>


### [134] [Riemannian Gradient Method with Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04078)
*Filippo Leggio,Diego Scuppa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种带动量项的黎曼梯度方法，用于黎曼流形上的光滑函数优化，具有O(ε⁻²)的最坏情况复杂度保证，在Manopt基准测试中表现出竞争性甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将最近在无约束优化中引入的动量方法扩展到黎曼流形优化问题，以提升优化算法的收敛性能和效率。

Method: 提出黎曼梯度动量方法，结合安全保护规则，是最近无约束优化动量方法的非平凡扩展，在黎曼流形上实现梯度下降与动量项的整合。

Result: 理论证明算法在最坏情况下以O(ε⁻²)复杂度达到ε-稳定点；在Manopt包基准问题上的大量计算实验显示，相比现有最优求解器具有竞争性且通常更优的性能。

Conclusion: 数值证据证实了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性，成功将动量方法扩展到黎曼优化领域，为黎曼流形上的优化问题提供了有意义的扩展。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of minimizing a smooth function on a Riemannian manifold and present a Riemannian gradient method with momentum. The proposed algorithm represents a substantial and nontrivial extension of a recently introduced method for unconstrained optimization. We prove that the algorithm, supported by a safeguarding rule, produces an $ε$-stationary point with a worst-case complexity bound of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark problems are carried out, comparing the proposed method with state-of-the-art solvers available in the Manopt package. The results demonstrate competitive and often superior performance. Overall, the numerical evidence confirms the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach, which provides a meaningful extension of the recently introduced momentum-based method to Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [135] [Lyapunov Stability of Stochastic Vector Optimization: Theory and Numerical Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04095)
*Thiago Santos,Sebastiao Xavier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于随机微分方程的向量优化漂移-扩散模型，通过Lyapunov分析建立了稳定性理论，并实现了与pymoo兼容的算法实现，在有限评估预算的高维多目标优化中表现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机微分方程在多目标优化中的应用受到两个主要限制：不完整的稳定性分析和缺乏可访问的实现。本文旨在填补这些空白，提供一个理论严谨且易于使用的随机漂移-扩散优化方法。

Method: 提出无约束向量优化的漂移-扩散模型，其中漂移项由共同下降方向诱导，扩散项保持探索行为。主要理论贡献是使用Lyapunov分析建立全局存在性、路径唯一性和非爆炸性（在耗散条件下），以及在额外强制假设下的正递归性。同时推导了Euler-Maruyama离散化，并将其实现为与pymoo兼容的算法，配有交互式PymooLab前端用于可重复实验。

Result: 在DTLZ2测试问题上（目标数量从3到15）的实证结果表明：与已建立的进化基线相比，该方法在低维情况下竞争力较弱，但在有限评估预算的高维设置中仍是一个可行的选择。这表明随机漂移-扩散搜索在数学可处理性方面占据了一个与基于群体的启发式方法并存的生态位。

Conclusion: 随机漂移-扩散搜索不是要替代基于群体的启发式方法，而是作为一种替代方案，其有利特性可以进行严格分析。该方法在数学可处理性和高维有限预算优化方面具有独特优势，为多目标优化提供了一个理论严谨且实用的工具。

Abstract: The use of stochastic differential equations in multi-objective optimization has been limited, in practice, by two persistent gaps: incomplete stability analyses and the absence of accessible implementations. We revisit a drift--diffusion model for unconstrained vector optimization in which the drift is induced by a common descent direction and the diffusion term preserves exploratory behavior. The main theoretical contribution is a self-contained Lyapunov analysis establishing global existence, pathwise uniqueness, and non-explosion under a dissipativity condition, together with positive recurrence under an additional coercivity assumption. We also derive an Euler--Maruyama discretization and implement the resulting iteration as a \emph{pymoo}-compatible algorithm -- \emph{pymoo} being an open-source Python framework for multi-objective optimization -- with an interactive \emph{PymooLab} front-end for reproducible experiments. Empirical results on DTLZ2 with objective counts from three to fifteen indicate a consistent trade-off: compared with established evolutionary baselines, the method is less competitive in low-dimensional regimes but remains a viable option under restricted evaluation budgets in higher-dimensional settings. Taken together, these observations suggest that stochastic drift--diffusion search occupies a mathematically tractable niche alongside population-based heuristics -- not as a replacement, but as an alternative whose favorable properties are amenable to rigorous analysis.

</details>


### [136] [Lyapunov characterization of boundedness of reachability sets for infinite-dimensional systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04190)
*Patrick Bachmann,Andrii Mironchenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了具有轨迹支配输入的Lipschitz连续流控制系统的可达集有界性的逆Lyapunov定理，并将其应用于半线性发展方程和常微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究控制系统中可达集有界性的Lyapunov理论，特别是对于具有轨迹支配输入的Lipschitz连续流系统，旨在建立更一般的逆Lyapunov定理框架。

Method: 采用逆Lyapunov定理方法，针对流在紧区间上关于轨迹支配输入具有Lipschitz连续性的控制系统，证明可达集有界性的充分必要条件。

Result: 证明了这类控制系统的可达集有界性逆Lyapunov定理，并表明该条件适用于许多半线性发展方程；对于常微分方程，得到了无输入幅度先验限制的前向完备性逆Lyapunov定理。

Conclusion: 该研究为具有轨迹支配输入的Lipschitz连续流控制系统建立了可达集有界性的逆Lyapunov理论框架，扩展了传统Lyapunov理论的应用范围，特别适用于半线性发展方程和常微分方程系统。

Abstract: We prove a converse Lyapunov theorem for boundedness of reachability sets for a general class of control systems whose flow is Lipschitz continuous on compact intervals with respect to trajectory-dominated inputs. We show that this condition is satisfied by many semi-linear evolution equations.
  For ordinary differential equations, as a consequence of our results, we obtain a converse Lyapunov theorem for forward completeness, without a priori restrictions on the magnitude of inputs.

</details>


### [137] [Optimal strategies in Markov decision processes with finitely additive evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04226)
*János Flesch,Arkadi Predtetchinski,William D Sudderth,Xavier Venel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究无限时域MDP中基于扩散电荷聚合期望阶段收益的最优策略存在性问题，通过反例证明在无额外假设时最优策略可能不存在


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neyman[2023]的研究表明，在满足时间价值原则的扩散电荷下，有限状态和动作空间的MDP存在纯最优策略。本文旨在探讨在没有额外假设的扩散电荷下，最优策略是否仍然存在。

Method: 通过构造一个精心设计的扩散电荷（扩散有限可加概率测度），构建一个反例MDP，证明在该设置下不存在任何最优策略（既无纯策略也无随机策略）。

Result: 证明了在没有额外假设的扩散电荷下，最优策略可能不存在。通过构造的反例显示，存在这样的MDP：无论纯策略还是随机策略，都无法达到最优。

Conclusion: Neyman[2023]关于扩散电荷MDP中纯最优策略存在的结论需要时间价值原则的额外假设。在无此假设的一般情况下，最优策略可能不存在，这为无限时域MDP中基于扩散电荷的决策理论提供了重要的理论边界。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon Markov decision processes (MDPs) where the decision maker evaluates each of her strategies by aggregating the infinite stream of expected stage-rewards. The crucial feature of our approach is that the aggregation is performed by means of a given diffuse charge (a diffuse finitely additive probability measure) on the set of stages. The results of Neyman [2023] imply that in this setting, in every MDP with finite state and action spaces, the decision maker has a pure optimal strategy as long as the diffuse charge satisfies the time value of money principle. His result raises the question of existence of an optimal strategy without additional assumptions on the aggregation charge. We answer this question in the negative with a counterexample. With a delicately constructed aggregation charge, the MDP has no optimal strategy at all, neither pure nor randomized.

</details>


### [138] [Maintenance optimization of a two-component system with mixed observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04280)
*Nan Zhang,Inmaculada T. Castro,M. L. Gamiz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究混合可观测性下双组件系统的维护优化问题，提出基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）的建模框架，并开发参数估计算法和最优维护策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中常存在混合可观测性：某些组件完全可监测，而其他组件因传感限制只能部分观测。此外，组件间可能存在单向退化依赖关系，即一个组件的健康状态会影响另一个组件的退化过程。现有维护策略往往忽略这些复杂特性，导致次优决策。

Method: 1. 采用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）对混合可观测系统进行建模；2. 在温和条件下，解析地建立最优维护策略的结构性质；3. 开发基于多样本路径的Baum-Welch算法，用于协变量依赖隐马尔可夫模型的参数估计；4. 通过数值实验验证参数估计和维护策略的有效性。

Result: 1. 理论证明了最优维护策略的结构性质；2. 提出的参数估计算法能有效从观测数据中学习系统参数；3. 在64个实例的数值实验中，所提维护策略始终优于经典的基于阈值的策略；4. 当组件U1退化较快时，最大成本降低可达约6%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的POMDP框架能有效处理混合可观测性和单向退化依赖的复杂系统维护问题。理论分析和数值实验均表明，该框架在参数估计和维护决策方面优于传统方法，为实际工业系统的智能维护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies maintenance optimization for a two-component system under mixed observability. Component~$U_1$ is fully monitored, whereas component~$U_2$ is only partially observable due to sensing limitations. The system exhibits unidirectional positive degradation dependence, in which the health state of component~$U_1$ influences the degradation process of component~$U_2$, but not vice versa. We propose a novel framework for modeling and optimizing maintenance decisions for such systems using a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP). Under mild conditions, we analytically establish structural properties of the optimal maintenance policy. Baum-Welch algorithm with multiple sample paths is developed to estimate the unknown system parameters in the context of a covariate-dependent Hidden Markov Model. %from observational data with multiple trajectories. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed parameter estimation and the maintenance policy. Across 64 instances, we show that it consistently outperforms classical threshold-based policies. Specifically, when the degradation of component $U_1$ is faster, it achieves maximal cost reductions of up to approximately $6\%$

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Build, Judge, Optimize: A Blueprint for Continuous Improvement of Multi-Agent Consumer Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03565)
*Alejandro Breen Herrera,Aayush Sheth,Steven G. Xu,Zhucheng Zhan,Charles Wright,Marcus Yearwood,Hongtai Wei,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估和优化对话购物助手的实用框架，针对多轮交互评估和多智能体系统优化的挑战，通过一个生产级AI杂货购物助手进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话购物助手从原型到生产面临两大挑战：如何评估多轮交互，以及如何优化紧密耦合的多智能体系统。杂货购物场景进一步加剧了这些困难，因为用户请求通常不完整、高度偏好敏感，且受预算和库存等因素约束。

Method: 1) 引入多维度评估框架，将端到端购物质量分解为结构化维度；2) 开发与人工标注对齐的校准LLM-as-judge流程；3) 基于GEPA提示优化器研究两种互补策略：Sub-agent GEPA（针对单个智能体节点优化）和MAMuT GEPA（系统级联合优化，使用多轮模拟和轨迹级评分）。

Result: 提出了一个实用的评估和优化蓝图，开发了校准的LLM评估流程，并展示了两种提示优化策略在优化对话购物助手性能方面的有效性。发布了评估模板和设计指南以支持实践者。

Conclusion: 本文为构建生产级对话购物助手提供了系统化的评估和优化方法，通过多维度评估框架和创新的提示优化策略，解决了多轮交互评估和多智能体系统优化的关键挑战，为实践者提供了实用工具和指导。

Abstract: Conversational shopping assistants (CSAs) represent a compelling application of agentic AI, but moving from prototype to production reveals two underexplored challenges: how to evaluate multi-turn interactions and how to optimize tightly coupled multi-agent systems. Grocery shopping further amplifies these difficulties, as user requests are often underspecified, highly preference-sensitive, and constrained by factors such as budget and inventory. In this paper, we present a practical blueprint for evaluating and optimizing conversational shopping assistants, illustrated through a production-scale AI grocery assistant. We introduce a multi-faceted evaluation rubric that decomposes end-to-end shopping quality into structured dimensions and develop a calibrated LLM-as-judge pipeline aligned with human annotations. Building on this evaluation foundation, we investigate two complementary prompt-optimization strategies based on a SOTA prompt-optimizer called GEPA (Shao et al., 2025): (1) Sub-agent GEPA, which optimizes individual agent nodes against localized rubrics, and (2) MAMuT (Multi-Agent Multi-Turn) GEPA (Herrera et al., 2026), a novel system-level approach that jointly optimizes prompts across agents using multi-turn simulation and trajectory-level scoring. We release rubric templates and evaluation design guidance to support practitioners building production CSAs.

</details>


### [140] [MAGE: Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Language Agents toward Strategic Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03680)
*Lu Yang,Zelai Xu,Minyang Xie,Jiaxuan Gao,Zhao Shok,Yu Wang,Yi Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGE是一个元强化学习框架，使LLM智能体能够在多智能体环境中进行战略探索和利用，通过多回合训练和基于种群的训练来提升适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在非平稳环境中适应能力有限，传统方法如上下文学习和外部记忆无法内化长期改进所需的适应能力。现有的元强化学习方法主要关注单智能体环境中的探索，忽略了多智能体环境中战略利用的重要性。

Method: MAGE采用多回合训练机制，将交互历史和反思整合到上下文窗口中，使用最终回合奖励作为目标来激励智能体基于过去经验优化策略。结合基于种群的训练和智能体特定的优势归一化技术，以丰富智能体多样性并确保稳定学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，MAGE在探索和利用任务上都优于现有基线方法。此外，MAGE对未见过的对手表现出强大的泛化能力，表明它已经内化了战略探索和利用的能力。

Conclusion: MAGE框架成功地将元强化学习应用于LLM智能体，使其能够在多智能体环境中进行有效的战略探索和利用，并展现出良好的泛化能力，为LLM智能体在动态环境中的长期改进提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in learned tasks, yet they often struggle to adapt to non-stationary environments with feedback. While In-Context Learning and external memory offer some flexibility, they fail to internalize the adaptive ability required for long-term improvement. Meta-Reinforcement Learning (meta-RL) provides an alternative by embedding the learning process directly within the model. However, existing meta-RL approaches for LLMs focus primarily on exploration in single-agent settings, neglecting the strategic exploitation necessary for multi-agent environments. We propose MAGE, a meta-RL framework that empowers LLM agents for strategic exploration and exploitation. MAGE utilizes a multi-episode training regime where interaction histories and reflections are integrated into the context window. By using the final episode reward as the objective, MAGE incentivizes the agent to refine its strategy based on past experiences. We further combine population-based training with an agent-specific advantage normalization technique to enrich agent diversity and ensure stable learning. Experiment results show that MAGE outperforms existing baselines in both exploration and exploitation tasks. Furthermore, MAGE exhibits strong generalization to unseen opponents, suggesting it has internalized the ability for strategic exploration and exploitation. Code is available at https://github.com/Lu-Yang666/MAGE.

</details>


### [141] [AI4S-SDS: A Neuro-Symbolic Solvent Design System via Sparse MCTS and Differentiable Physics Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03686)
*Jiangyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI4S-SDS：一种用于化学配方自动化设计的神经符号框架，通过多智能体协作与蒙特卡洛树搜索，结合稀疏状态存储和全局-局部搜索策略，在固定token预算下实现深度探索和多样性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学配方自动化设计面临高维组合空间的挑战，现有LLM智能体存在上下文窗口限制和路径依赖探索导致的模式崩溃问题，需要开发能够进行深度探索并保持多样性的框架。

Method: 提出AI4S-SDS神经符号框架：1）稀疏状态存储与动态路径重建机制，将推理历史与上下文长度解耦；2）全局-局部搜索策略，包括基于历史反馈的自适应搜索根重配置和兄弟感知扩展机制；3）可微分物理引擎，使用混合归一化损失和稀疏诱导正则化优化连续混合比例。

Result: AI4S-SDS在采用的HSP物理约束下实现完全有效性，相比基线智能体显著提高探索多样性。在初步光刻实验中，框架发现了一种新型光刻胶显影剂配方，其性能与商业基准相当或更优。

Conclusion: AI4S-SDS展示了多样性驱动的神经符号搜索在科学发现中的潜力，能够有效解决化学配方设计中的高维组合优化问题，为材料科学中的自动化设计提供了新方法。

Abstract: Automated design of chemical formulations is a cornerstone of materials science, yet it requires navigating a high-dimensional combinatorial space involving discrete compositional choices and continuous geometric constraints. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) agents face significant challenges in this setting, including context window limitations during long-horizon reasoning and path-dependent exploration that may lead to mode collapse. To address these issues, we introduce AI4S-SDS, a closed-loop neuro-symbolic framework that integrates multi-agent collaboration with a tailored Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) engine. We propose a Sparse State Storage mechanism with Dynamic Path Reconstruction, which decouples reasoning history from context length and enables arbitrarily deep exploration under fixed token budgets. To reduce local convergence and improve coverage, we implement a Global--Local Search Strategy: a memory-driven planning module adaptively reconfigures the search root based on historical feedback, while a Sibling-Aware Expansion mechanism promotes orthogonal exploration at the node level. Furthermore, we bridge symbolic reasoning and physical feasibility through a Differentiable Physics Engine, employing a hybrid normalized loss with sparsity-inducing regularization to optimize continuous mixing ratios under thermodynamic constraints. Empirical results show that AI4S-SDS achieves full validity under the adopted HSP-based physical constraints and substantially improves exploration diversity compared to baseline agents. In preliminary lithography experiments, the framework identifies a novel photoresist developer formulation that demonstrates competitive or superior performance relative to a commercial benchmark, highlighting the potential of diversity-driven neuro-symbolic search for scientific discovery.

</details>


### [142] [RAGNav: A Retrieval-Augmented Topological Reasoning Framework for Multi-Goal Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03745)
*Ling Luo,Qiangian Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGNav框架通过双基记忆系统（拓扑地图+语义森林）和锚点引导检索机制，解决了多目标视觉语言导航中的空间幻觉和规划漂移问题，实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标视觉语言导航任务需要同时识别多个实体并推理其空间物理约束和执行顺序，但传统的检索增强生成方法缺乏显式空间建模，导致空间幻觉和规划漂移问题。

Method: 提出RAGNav框架，核心是双基记忆系统：低层拓扑地图维护物理连通性，高层语义森林进行层次化环境抽象。基于此表示，引入锚点引导条件检索和拓扑邻居分数传播机制，快速筛选候选目标并消除语义噪声，利用拓扑邻域的物理关联进行语义校准。

Result: 实验结果表明RAGNav在复杂多目标导航任务中实现了最先进的性能，显著提升了目标间可达性推理能力和顺序规划效率。

Conclusion: RAGNav通过桥接语义推理与物理结构，有效解决了多目标视觉语言导航中的空间建模不足问题，为复杂导航任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) is evolving from single-point pathfinding toward the more challenging Multi-Goal VLN. This task requires agents to accurately identify multiple entities while collaboratively reasoning over their spatial-physical constraints and sequential execution order. However, generic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) paradigms often suffer from spatial hallucinations and planning drift when handling multi-object associations due to the lack of explicit spatial modeling.To address these challenges, we propose RAGNav, a framework that bridges the gap between semantic reasoning and physical structure. The core of RAGNav is a Dual-Basis Memory system, which integrates a low-level topological map for maintaining physical connectivity with a high-level semantic forest for hierarchical environment abstraction. Building on this representation, the framework introduces an anchor-guided conditional retrieval and a topological neighbor score propagation mechanism. This approach facilitates the rapid screening of candidate targets and the elimination of semantic noise, while performing semantic calibration by leveraging the physical associations inherent in the topological neighborhood.This mechanism significantly enhances the capability of inter-target reachability reasoning and the efficiency of sequential planning. Experimental results demonstrate that RAGNav achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in complex multi-goal navigation tasks.

</details>


### [143] [AgentSelect: Benchmark for Narrative Query-to-Agent Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03761)
*Yunxiao Shi,Wujiang Xu,Tingwei Chen,Haoning Shang,Ling Yang,Yunfeng Wan,Zhuo Cao,Xing Zi,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSelect是一个用于LLM智能体推荐的基准测试，通过将异构评估数据统一为查询到智能体的交互记录，解决了现有评估方法碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体生态系统缺乏系统化的配置选择方法，现有评估方法（如排行榜和基准测试）在任务、指标和候选池方面存在碎片化，无法为端到端智能体配置推荐提供查询条件化的监督。

Method: 将智能体选择重新定义为基于能力配置的叙事查询到智能体推荐，系统地将异构评估数据转换为统一的、仅包含正向交互的数据。构建了包含111,179个查询、107,721个可部署智能体和251,103个交互记录的基准数据集。

Result: 分析揭示了从密集头部重用到长尾、近乎一次性监督的机制转变，表明基于流行度的协同过滤方法变得脆弱，需要内容感知的能力匹配。合成的组合交互是可学习的，在受控反事实编辑下诱导能力敏感行为，并提高了对现实组合的覆盖度。

Conclusion: AgentSelect为智能体推荐提供了首个统一的数据和评估基础设施，为研究和加速新兴智能体生态系统建立了可复现的基础。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly becoming the practical interface for task automation, yet the ecosystem lacks a principled way to choose among an exploding space of deployable configurations. Existing LLM leaderboards and tool/agent benchmarks evaluate components in isolation and remain fragmented across tasks, metrics, and candidate pools, leaving a critical research gap: there is little query-conditioned supervision for learning to recommend end-to-end agent configurations that couple a backbone model with a toolkit. We address this gap with AgentSelect, a benchmark that reframes agent selection as narrative query-to-agent recommendation over capability profiles and systematically converts heterogeneous evaluation artifacts into unified, positive-only interaction data. AgentSelectcomprises 111,179 queries, 107,721 deployable agents, and 251,103 interaction records aggregated from 40+ sources, spanning LLM-only, toolkit-only, and compositional agents. Our analyses reveal a regime shift from dense head reuse to long-tail, near one-off supervision, where popularity-based CF/GNN methods become fragile and content-aware capability matching is essential. We further show that Part~III synthesized compositional interactions are learnable, induce capability-sensitive behavior under controlled counterfactual edits, and improve coverage over realistic compositions; models trained on AgentSelect also transfer to a public agent marketplace (MuleRun), yielding consistent gains on an unseen catalog. Overall, AgentSelect provides the first unified data and evaluation infrastructure for agent recommendation, which establishes a reproducible foundation to study and accelerate the emerging agent ecosystem.

</details>


### [144] [Specification-Driven Generation and Evaluation of Discrete-Event World Models via the DEVS Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03784)
*Zheyu Chen,Zhuohuan Li,Chuanhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合显式模拟器可靠性与学习模型灵活性的离散事件世界模型生成方法，通过LLM从自然语言规范直接合成可执行的DEVS模型，支持在线执行期间的动态适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型存在两极分化：手工设计的模拟器具有一致性和可复现性但难以适应变化，而隐式神经模型灵活但难以约束、验证和调试。需要一种结合两者优点的中间方案，使世界模型能够在在线执行期间适应变化。

Method: 采用DEVS形式化方法，引入分阶段的LLM生成管道，将组件交互的结构推断与组件级事件和时序逻辑分离。模型从自然语言规范直接合成可执行的离散事件世界模型，通过结构化事件轨迹验证规范派生的时间和语义约束。

Result: 生成的世界模型在长时程推演中保持一致性，可通过可观测行为进行验证，并在在线执行期间能够高效按需合成。适用于排队和服务操作、具身任务规划、消息介导的多智能体协调等离散事件驱动的环境。

Conclusion: 该方法在显式模拟器的可靠性与学习模型的灵活性之间找到了原则性的中间地带，实现了可验证、可调试且能在在线执行期间动态适应的世界模型，为智能体系统的规划和评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models are essential for planning and evaluation in agentic systems, yet existing approaches lie at two extremes: hand-engineered simulators that offer consistency and reproducibility but are costly to adapt, and implicit neural models that are flexible but difficult to constrain, verify, and debug over long horizons. We seek a principled middle ground that combines the reliability of explicit simulators with the flexibility of learned models, allowing world models to be adapted during online execution. By targeting a broad class of environments whose dynamics are governed by the ordering, timing, and causality of discrete events, such as queueing and service operations, embodied task planning, and message-mediated multi-agent coordination, we advocate explicit, executable discrete-event world models synthesized directly from natural-language specifications. Our approach adopts the DEVS formalism and introduces a staged LLM-based generation pipeline that separates structural inference of component interactions from component-level event and timing logic. To evaluate generated models without a unique ground truth, simulators emit structured event traces that are validated against specification-derived temporal and semantic constraints, enabling reproducible verification and localized diagnostics. Together, these contributions produce world models that are consistent over long-horizon rollouts, verifiable from observable behavior, and efficient to synthesize on demand during online execution.

</details>


### [145] [A Rubric-Supervised Critic from Sparse Real-World Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03800)
*Xingyao Wang,Valerie Chen,Heng Ji,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种从稀疏、噪声的人类-智能体交互数据中学习"批评家"模型的方法，用于改进编码智能体的评估和训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术基准主要奖励自主任务完成（如单元测试通过），但现实世界编码智能体与人类协作时，成功信号通常具有噪声、延迟和稀疏性，需要弥合这一差距

Method: 提出Critic Rubrics框架，包含24个行为特征，仅从人类-智能体交互轨迹中提取；采用半监督目标联合预测这些评分标准和稀疏的人类反馈

Result: 在SWE-bench上，批评家模型显著改进最佳N重排序（Best@8比Random@8提升15.9%），支持早期停止（减少83%尝试次数下提升17.7%），并支持训练时数据筛选

Conclusion: 通过从稀疏、噪声的交互数据中学习批评家模型，可以有效弥合学术基准与现实世界编码智能体评估之间的差距，提升智能体性能

Abstract: Academic benchmarks for coding agents tend to reward autonomous task completion, measured by verifiable rewards such as unit-test success. In contrast, real-world coding agents operate with humans in the loop, where success signals are typically noisy, delayed, and sparse. How can we bridge this gap? In this paper, we propose a process to learn a "critic" model from sparse and noisy interaction data, which can then be used both as a reward model for either RL-based training or inference-time scaling. Specifically, we introduce Critic Rubrics, a rubric-based supervision framework with 24 behavioral features that can be derived from human-agent interaction traces alone. Using a semi-supervised objective, we can then jointly predict these rubrics and sparse human feedback (when present). In experiments, we demonstrate that, despite being trained primarily from trace-observable rubrics and sparse real-world outcome proxies, these critics improve best-of-N reranking on SWE-bench (Best@8 +15.9 over Random@8 over the rerankable subset of trajectories), enable early stopping (+17.7 with 83% fewer attempts), and support training-time data curation via critic-selected trajectories.

</details>


### [146] [Generative AI in Managerial Decision-Making: Redefining Boundaries through Ambiguity Resolution and Sycophancy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03970)
*Sule Ozturk Birim,Fabrizio Marozzo,Yigit Kazancoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了生成式AI在商业模糊性检测、解决和顺从行为方面的表现，发现AI能有效检测内部矛盾和语境模糊，但在语言结构细微差别上表现不佳，需要人类监督确保战略可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正被整合到复杂商业流程中，但其在模糊商业情境中提供战略建议的可靠性仍存在关键知识缺口。需要评估AI在模糊性检测、解决过程中的表现及其对错误指令的顺从行为。

Method: 使用新颖的四维商业模糊性分类法，在战略、战术和操作场景中进行人机协同实验。通过"LLM-as-a-judge"框架评估决策质量，包括一致性、可操作性、论证质量和约束遵守等标准。

Result: 模型在检测内部矛盾和语境模糊方面表现出色，但在结构语言细微差别上表现不佳。模糊性解决过程持续提高了所有决策类型的响应质量。顺从行为分析显示不同模型架构呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究将GAI定位为认知支架，能够检测和解决管理者可能忽略的模糊性，但其自身的人工局限性需要人类管理来确保其作为战略伙伴的可靠性，为有限理性文献做出贡献。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence is increasingly being integrated into complex business workflows, fundamentally shifting the boundaries of managerial decision-making. However, the reliability of its strategic advice in ambiguous business contexts remains a critical knowledge gap. This study addresses this by comparing various models on ambiguity detection, evaluating how a systematic resolution process enhances response quality, and investigating their sycophantic behavior when presented with flawed directives. Using a novel four-dimensional business ambiguity taxonomy, we conducted a human-in-the-loop experiment across strategic, tactical, and operational scenarios. The resulting decisions were assessed with an "LLM-as-a-judge" framework on criteria including agreement, actionability, justification quality, and constraint adherence. Results reveal distinct performance capabilities. While models excel in detecting internal contradictions and contextual ambiguities, they struggle with structural linguistic nuances. Ambiguity resolution consistently increased response quality across all decision types, while sycophantic behavior analysis revealed distinct patterns depending on the model architecture. This study contributes to the bounded rationality literature by positioning GAI as a cognitive scaffold that can detect and resolve ambiguities managers might overlook, but whose own artificial limitations necessitate human management to ensure its reliability as a strategic partner.

</details>


### [147] [Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03975)
*Jyoti Aneja,Michael Harrison,Neel Joshi,Tyler LaBonte,John Langford,Eduardo Salinas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B是一个紧凑的开源多模态推理模型，通过精心架构设计和严格数据筛选，在较少计算资源下实现竞争性性能，特别擅长科学数学推理和UI理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究社区提供构建更小、更高效多模态推理模型的实用见解，并分享一个在通用视觉语言任务上表现良好，在科学数学推理和用户界面理解方面表现优异的开源模型。

Method: 采用系统化数据筛选、纠错和合成增强；使用高分辨率动态分辨率编码器；混合推理和非推理数据，配合显式模式标记，使单一模型既能快速直接回答简单任务，又能进行复杂问题的链式思考推理。

Result: 证明精心架构选择和严格数据筛选能使较小的开源多模态模型以显著更少的训练和推理计算资源及token数量实现竞争性性能。数据质量是模型性能的主要杠杆，高分辨率编码器带来一致改进。

Conclusion: 数据质量是多模态模型性能的关键因素，高分辨率感知是高质量推理的前提，混合推理模式设计使单一模型能灵活应对不同复杂度任务，为构建高效紧凑多模态推理模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We present Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B, a compact open-weight multimodal reasoning model, and share the motivations, design choices, experiments, and learnings that informed its development. Our goal is to contribute practical insight to the research community on building smaller, efficient multimodal reasoning models and to share the result of these learnings as an open-weight model that is good at common vision and language tasks and excels at scientific and mathematical reasoning and understanding user interfaces. Our contributions include demonstrating that careful architecture choices and rigorous data curation enable smaller, open-weight multimodal models to achieve competitive performance with significantly less training and inference-time compute and tokens. The most substantial improvements come from systematic filtering, error correction, and synthetic augmentation -- reinforcing that data quality remains the primary lever for model performance. Systematic ablations show that high-resolution, dynamic-resolution encoders yield consistent improvements, as accurate perception is a prerequisite for high-quality reasoning. Finally, a hybrid mix of reasoning and non-reasoning data with explicit mode tokens allows a single model to deliver fast direct answers for simpler tasks and chain-of-thought reasoning for complex problems.

</details>


### [148] [BeamPERL: Parameter-Efficient RL with Verifiable Rewards Specializes Compact LLMs for Structured Beam Mechanics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04124)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用硬性可验证奖励的强化学习能教会紧凑语言模型进行物理推理吗？研究发现，在梁静力学问题上，RLVR训练使模型性能提升66.7%，但学习到的能力是各向异性的：能组合泛化但无法处理拓扑变化，表明奖励对齐仅诱导了程序化模板而非对物理方程的内在理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习与硬性可验证奖励是否能真正教会紧凑语言模型进行物理推理，还是仅仅学习模式匹配正确答案。通过梁静力学这一经典工程问题，研究在没有教师生成推理轨迹的情况下，仅使用符号求解器提供的二元正确性奖励进行参数高效RLVR训练的效果。

Method: 使用1.5B参数推理模型，在梁静力学问题上进行参数高效的RLVR（强化学习与可验证奖励）训练。仅使用符号求解器提供的二元正确性奖励，没有教师生成的推理轨迹。通过BeamPERL方法训练，分析模型在不同检查点的表现。

Result: 最佳BeamPERL检查点在Pass@1指标上比基础模型提升66.7%。但学习到的能力是各向异性的：模型能组合泛化（更多载荷），但在需要相同平衡方程的拓扑变化（移动支撑）下失败。中间检查点产生最强的推理能力，而持续优化会降低鲁棒性同时保持奖励。这表明结果级对齐存在关键限制。

Conclusion: 强化学习与精确物理奖励仅诱导了程序化解决方案模板，而非对控制方程的内在理解。即使奖励信号在分析上是精确的，其本身也不能保证可迁移的物理推理。可验证奖励可能需要与结构化推理支架相结合，才能超越模板匹配，实现稳健的科学推理。

Abstract: Can reinforcement learning with hard, verifiable rewards teach a compact language model to reason about physics, or does it primarily learn to pattern-match toward correct answers? We study this question by training a 1.5B-parameter reasoning model on beam statics, a classic engineering problem, using parameter-efficient RLVR with binary correctness rewards from symbolic solvers, without teacher-generated reasoning traces. The best BeamPERL checkpoint achieves a 66.7% improvement in Pass@1 over the base model. However, the learned competence is anisotropic: the model generalizes compositionally (more loads) but fails under topological shifts (moved supports) that require the same equilibrium equations. Intermediate checkpoints yield the strongest reasoning, while continued optimization degrades robustness while maintaining reward. These findings reveal a key limitation of outcome-level alignment: reinforcement learning with exact physics rewards induces procedural solution templates rather than internalization of governing equations. The precision of the reward signal - even when analytically exact - does not by itself guarantee transferable physical reasoning. Our results suggest that verifiable rewards may need to be paired with structured reasoning scaffolding to move beyond template matching toward robust scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Realistic Personalization: Evaluating Long-Horizon Preference Following in Personalized User-LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04191)
*Qianyun Guo,Yibo Li,Yue Liu,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RealPref是一个评估LLM在个性化用户交互中遵循用户偏好的基准测试，包含100个用户档案、1300个偏好、四种偏好表达方式和长时程交互历史，结果显示LLM性能随上下文增长和偏好表达隐晦而显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地作为个人助手使用，用户会在长期交互中分享复杂多样的偏好。然而，评估LLM在现实、长期情境中遵循这些偏好的能力仍然未被充分探索，需要建立一个更贴近真实场景的评估基准。

Method: 提出了RealPref基准测试，包含100个用户档案、1300个个性化偏好、四种偏好表达方式（从显式到隐式）和长时程交互历史。采用三种测试问题类型（多项选择、是非判断、开放式问题），并使用LLM-as-a-judge评估方法配合详细评分标准。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）随着上下文长度增加，LLM性能显著下降；2）偏好表达越隐晦，LLM性能越差；3）将用户偏好理解泛化到未见场景面临额外挑战。这些发现揭示了当前LLM在长期个性化交互中的局限性。

Conclusion: RealPref基准测试为未来研究提供了基础，有助于开发更能适应用户个体需求的用户感知型LLM助手。该基准突出了当前LLM在长期个性化交互中的不足，为改进方向提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly serving as personal assistants, where users share complex and diverse preferences over extended interactions. However, assessing how well LLMs can follow these preferences in realistic, long-term situations remains underexplored. This work proposes RealPref, a benchmark for evaluating realistic preference-following in personalized user-LLM interactions. RealPref features 100 user profiles, 1300 personalized preferences, four types of preference expression (ranging from explicit to implicit), and long-horizon interaction histories. It includes three types of test questions (multiple-choice, true-or-false, and open-ended), with detailed rubrics for LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. Results indicate that LLM performance significantly drops as context length grows and preference expression becomes more implicit, and that generalizing user preference understanding to unseen scenarios poses further challenges. RealPref and these findings provide a foundation for future research to develop user-aware LLM assistants that better adapt to individual needs. The code is available at https://github.com/GG14127/RealPref.

</details>


### [150] [Agentics 2.0: Logical Transduction Algebra for Agentic Data Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04241)
*Alfio Massimiliano Gliozzo,Junkyu Lee,Nahuel Defosse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentics 2.0是一个轻量级Python框架，用于构建高质量、结构化、可解释的类型安全智能体数据工作流，通过逻辑转导代数将LLM推理形式化为类型化语义转换，实现语义可靠性、可观测性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正从研究原型转向企业部署，需要满足可靠性、可扩展性和可观测性等软件质量属性，而不仅仅是文本生成。现有框架缺乏对结构化、类型安全和可解释工作流的支持。

Method: 提出逻辑转导代数，将LLM推理调用形式化为类型化语义转换（转导函数），确保模式有效性和证据局部性。转导函数通过代数算子组合成更大程序，以无状态异步调用在异步Map-Reduce程序中并行执行。

Result: 在DiscoveryBench（数据驱动发现）和Archer（NL-to-SQL语义解析）等挑战性基准测试中展示了最先进的性能。框架提供语义可靠性（强类型）、语义可观测性（输入输出类型槽间的证据追踪）和可扩展性（无状态并行执行）。

Conclusion: Agentics 2.0通过形式化的逻辑转导代数和类型安全机制，为企业级智能体AI部署提供了可靠、可扩展、可观测的框架解决方案，成功将研究原型转化为满足企业质量要求的实用系统。

Abstract: Agentic AI is rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to enterprise deployments, where requirements extend to meet the software quality attributes of reliability, scalability, and observability beyond plausible text generation. We present Agentics 2.0, a lightweight, Python-native framework for building high-quality, structured, explainable, and type-safe agentic data workflows. At the core of Agentics 2.0, the logical transduction algebra formalizes a large language model inference call as a typed semantic transformation, which we call a transducible function that enforces schema validity and the locality of evidence. The transducible functions compose into larger programs via algebraically grounded operators and execute as stateless asynchronous calls in parallel in asynchronous Map-Reduce programs. The proposed framework provides semantic reliability through strong typing, semantic observability through evidence tracing between slots of the input and output types, and scalability through stateless parallel execution. We instantiate reusable design patterns and evaluate the programs in Agentics 2.0 on challenging benchmarks, including DiscoveryBench for data-driven discovery and Archer for NL-to-SQL semantic parsing, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [151] [$τ$-Knowledge: Evaluating Conversational Agents over Unstructured Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04370)
*Quan Shi,Alexandra Zytek,Pedram Razavi,Karthik Narasimhan,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: τ-Knowledge扩展了τ-Bench，用于评估在需要协调外部自然语言知识与工具输出的环境中工作的智能体，特别针对金融科技客户支持场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多独立评估检索或工具使用，缺乏对在非结构化数据上进行长时程交互的完全自主智能体的现实评估，这在知识密集型部署中至关重要

Method: 引入τ-Knowledge作为τ-Bench的扩展，创建τ-Banking领域模拟现实金融科技客户支持工作流，智能体需在约700个互连知识文档中导航，同时执行工具介导的账户更新

Result: 即使具有高推理预算的前沿模型，在基于嵌入的检索和基于终端的搜索中，通过率仅约25.5%，且在重复试验中可靠性急剧下降；智能体难以从密集互连知识库中检索正确文档，并对复杂内部政策进行准确推理

Conclusion: τ-Knowledge为开发在人类面向部署中集成非结构化知识的智能体提供了现实的测试平台，揭示了当前智能体在协调外部知识与工具输出方面的显著局限性

Abstract: Conversational agents are increasingly deployed in knowledge-intensive settings, where correct behavior depends on retrieving and applying domain-specific knowledge from large, proprietary, and unstructured corpora during live interactions with users. Yet most existing benchmarks evaluate retrieval or tool use independently of each other, creating a gap in realistic, fully agentic evaluation over unstructured data in long-horizon interactions. We introduce $τ$-Knowledge, an extension of $τ$-Bench for evaluating agents in environments where success depends on coordinating external, natural-language knowledge with tool outputs to produce verifiable, policy-compliant state changes. Our new domain, $τ$-Banking, models realistic fintech customer support workflows in which agents must navigate roughly 700 interconnected knowledge documents while executing tool-mediated account updates. Across embedding-based retrieval and terminal-based search, even frontier models with high reasoning budgets achieve only $\sim$25.5% pass^1, with reliability degrading sharply over repeated trials. Agents struggle to retrieve the correct documents from densely interlinked knowledge bases and to reason accurately over complex internal policies. Overall, $τ$-Knowledge provides a realistic testbed for developing agents that integrate unstructured knowledge in human-facing deployments.

</details>
